Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in harmonic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 818 and the invariant I(A,B) = 718 mod 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the harmonic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^818 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 718-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
